# STYLE-2-PULSE — THE WORLD PULSE'S NEWS, RECEIPT AND EVENT PROSE AND THE MAP'S WORDS, BLOCK BY BLOCK, AGAINST THE OWNER'S STYLE LAW (2026-11-09)

**The owner, 2026-11-09, verbatim:** *"Check everywhere in the dossier that you created to see if the style law exists or not and correct"*. **The law:** [`../2026-11-07/WIRING-RULINGS-2026-11-07.md`](../2026-11-07/WIRING-RULINGS-2026-11-07.md) §5–§8 and [`../2026-11-07/STYLE-RULINGS-2026-11-07.md`](../2026-11-07/STYLE-RULINGS-2026-11-07.md) §1–§2. In short: evidence a visitor would notice before any judgment; the event, the people, the consequence and the season ahead, said plainly; no machinery; the labels stay; flavour in the intro positions, four vantages per intro pool; plain and concrete, never plain and generic; the retired word "faction" → "power".

**Seat:** STYLE-2-PULSE-R2 (Seat C, PULSE, of `BRIEF-STYLE-2-AUDIT-2026-11-09.md` §2b), on Opus 5.5, re-run as a fresh seat after the first PULSE seat's push was refused. A child of the cloud chair (`session_01FbPLbejcfwX5nii12VM673`), in session `session_01FbPLbejQX8syPTFZySqHjh`.

**READ-ONLY ON THE PRODUCT.** This file and the roster bump it needs (`tests/lint/.source-citation-baseline.json`, the walker's header line) are the only bytes the lane writes. Every PROPOSED line below is a proposal, never applied.

**Answer, first:** the law does **not** hold in the pulse. Of **929 block types judged, 269 PASS and 646 REWRITE** (plus 11 code defects and 3 lines already cured on SW-3's lane).
- **The news card, as it prints, carries almost no fact on its face.** Every one of the 4,496 measured card bodies is a lifecycle line with no event in it ("It is being felt {scope} now."), while the event, the source, the road and the goods sit only in the hover tooltip; every card wears a pipeline-state pill ("applied", "queued", "expired").
- **The most-printed headline names nothing:** "A far settlement feels the trouble spreading from town to town" (1,084 times as a frame; 1,292 with its variants), with the same phrase again as a pill.
- **Machinery is the dominant failure:** machinery-leak is carried by 364 rows: rule documentation as reasons ("An ally faces a gated obligation to mirror the hostility…", 436 prints), state arrows ("covert → revealed"), the strategy chooser, raw settlement ids ("Cold-war third party: j."), enum dumps ("Role: civic; goal: win public legitimacy."), design notes as pills ("The spatial substrate distributed the calamity toll…").
- **Single-variant leads everywhere:** no lead pool in the world-pulse writers meets four vantages except the TR-1 commerce corpus and the GR-0 lapse/default pools; every headline and summary row of A2 and at least 73 named lead rows in B, C1, C1c and C2 carry `needs: vantages` (almost all at 1 of 4).
- **The retired word:** 90 printed tokens in the measured run (56 on card faces, 34 in the tooltip; 11 of the face tokens are the crier line SW-3 cured on its lane), led by "Weak legitimacy gives cold-war rivals a proxy faction opening." (34 cards, printed twice on each). In source: 41 tokens in the world pulse's and the map's prose literals; 28 of them in prose that prints and 4 in labels, by the auditors' reading.

## §0 Method, base, the measurement

- **Base.** Branch `lane/style-2-pulse-r2-2026-11-09`, cut at `2053a809c` (`origin/lane/integration-2026-11-07`, MERGE-WAVE's final tip; the sha verified with `git rev-parse`). The clone had no `node_modules`; `npm ci` was run (exit 0; lockfile and tree unchanged).
- **The brief and STYLE-RULINGS-2 are not on any pushed ref** (`git ls-remote` and `git ls-tree` over every `lane/style-2-*`, `lane/chair-*` and the integration branch). The seat worked from the law at WIRING-RULINGS §5–§8 and STYLE-RULINGS §1–§2 (on base), the two sibling audits (`STYLE-AUDIT-2-SURFACES` on `lane/style-2-surfaces-2026-11-09` for the table's form; `STYLE-AUDIT-2-SOURCES` on `lane/style-2-sources-2026-11-09`, whose §4a hands this seat `src/domain/worldPulse` (2,923 literals in 205 files) and `src/components/map` (305 in 42)), and SW-3's record.
- **SW-3 REALM-PROSE is not in this base.** `git merge-base --is-ancestor d96ae7a41 2053a809c` is false. Its record (`lane/style-2-realm-prose-2026-11-09 :: docs/chair/2026-11-09/STYLE-2-REALM-PROSE-2026-11-09.md` §4a) was read and its 169 rows were not re-audited: its files are display/data files (newsVoice, newsBody 2 rows, decreeProsePools, …), none under `worldPulse`. Where a block here is one SW-3 rewrote, the row says `cured on SW-3's lane`. The crier quote line (`src/domain/display/newsVoice.js :: newsVoiceLine`) is SW-3's and is counted, not judged.
- **The render (the pulse on a mapped campaign).** A scratch copy of `scripts/audit/simulate-world.mjs` (imports made absolute; one line added to dump `wizardNews`, `worldState` and the saves at the end of each world; nothing in the repo touched) ran `--ticks 52 --reps 2 --scales 0` with `WAR_ECON_ALL=1`: two 14-town everything-on worlds (the war layer, strategy, religion, every organic system), 52 one-week ticks each through `src/domain/worldPulse/advanceCampaignWorld.js :: advanceCampaignWorld`, exit 0, `HEALTH: OK (no flags)`. **4,496 news entries** (2,018 + 2,478).
- **The card, as it prints.** Every entry was rendered through exactly what `src/components/map/WizardNewsPanel.jsx :: NewsEntry` prints: `entry.headline`, `newsBody.js :: newsBodyText` (the body), `newsVoice.js :: newsVoiceLine` (the quote), `newsBody.js :: newsReasonPhrases` (the first three reason pills) and `newsReaderSummary` (the tooltip): **24,209 printed lines**. Names were slotted to `{N}` and digits to `#` to fold frames: **332 block types (field × impact kind), 1,827 frames**. Each frame was traced to its writer by a fixed fragment of 12+ characters: 1,431 frames traced at once, the 451 left were traced by auditor A1 (UNTRACED: none).
- **Limits of the render.** The fixture's towns carry one-letter ids, so a code path that prints an id instead of a name shows as "j."; the path is real (rows A1·42, A2·22, the defects), the look is the fixture's. One world shape lights what it lights: only one receipt pool (`NPC_GOAL_NEWS`) and two map blocks printed; every other row is `dark`, a lower bound, judged from the writer and its consumer. No rumour ledger is consulted; the war receipt pools print in the war-reasons panel (`WarCausalBrief`), not through the rumour pools (auditor B corrected the seat's brief here).
- **The source inventory.** One scratch script (Babel parse, comments excluded) over `src/domain/worldPulse` and `src/components/map`: **5,802 prose-shaped literals (3+ words) in 325 files**, 41 "faction(s)/factional" tokens in them. A second count over the annexes `docs/content/RECEIPT_POOLS_*.md` finds the word 532 times, most in declarations and editorial notes; the pools are ANNEX-VERBATIM, so each receipt-pool REWRITE names its annex block.
- **The auditors.** Nine Opus block auditors under one brief (scratch `AUDITOR-BRIEF.md`: the law, the codes, the census law, the unit, the output shape), read-only, each writing one ledger:
  - **A1** the news card (newsBody, region/wizardNews, the eight most-printed pulse writers) and the 451 untraced frames;
  - **A2** the other 30 printed pulse writers;
  - **B** the realm's receipt pools and their pickers (23 files);
  - **C1, C1b, C1c** the dark pulse remainder, first half (89 files; C1 triaged all and reached 18, C1b and C1c took the 55 it named);
  - **C2** the dark pulse remainder, second half (89 files);
  - **D** the map's words (86 files), and **D2** a confirming whole-file read of the 56 map files D had ruled from the literal inventory (25 of 26 rows CONFIRMED, D·106's group PASS reversed for 10 of its 35 files, 20 rows added).
- **The unit.** A block is one template position (one pool, or one headline / summary / reason writer for one event kind, or one surface position on the map), judged once across every variant and town it prints in. Each row quotes the OLD text verbatim, cites `path :: symbol` and the line with its text, gives PASS, PASS (label) or REWRITE with the codes, and for REWRITE a PROPOSED line slotted only with the writer's own slots (the census law).
- **Codes:** `aphorism · mannered · mechanism-first · machinery-leak · no-evidence · duplicate-surface · retired-word · disconnected`, plus `defect` for a code defect found in passing. A contradiction is `no-evidence` with "contradicts:" in `needs`.
- **Row ids** carry the ledger (A1·4, B·65, C1c·57, D2·8): each ledger numbers on its own. `SCRATCH/` in a ledger's method notes is the seat's scratch directory; nothing there is committed.

## §5 CONFIRMED against PLAUSIBLE

- **CONFIRMED** (executed here): the base sha; the render (exit 0, 4,496 entries, HEALTH OK); the card text as `NewsEntry` composes it (the same four display functions called on every entry); the printed counts and frames (`cards.json`, `frames2.json`); the 41-token source count; that SW-3's rows are not in this base; the roster walker's count at this tip (the walkers' run, below).
- **PLAUSIBLE**: every verdict and every PROPOSED line is an auditor's reading against the law, not a measured outcome; the dark rows' reach (which world shape lights them) is read from the writer and its consumer, not printed; rows in files read by region (§4) stand until a whole read; the frame counts fold names by a roster of the run's own nouns, so a few frames split on names outside it (A2 counted from `cards.json` directly for that reason).

## §6 The ledgers

Each ledger follows, as its auditor wrote it: method notes, the table, its counts, its worst five, its exclusions, and what it did not reach.

### Ledger A1: THE NEWS CARD — body composer (newsBody.js), region news composer (wizardNews.js), and the most-printed world-pulse writers

- **Opened whole:** `src/domain/display/newsBody.js`, `src/domain/region/wizardNews.js`, `relationshipRulesCore.js`, `heraldDeeds.js`, `deityStanceLane.js`, `stressorAftermath.js`. **Opened by every prose region and every string literal** (comments skipped): `religiousContest.js` (all code lines), `warHomeCosts.js` (300–613 read whole, 1–299 is tuning and maths, no prose), `relationshipRulesAdversarial.js` (all 32 literals, 150–215 and 330–360 read whole), `mobilizationEffects.js` (140–218 whole, the only prose). The consumer was checked at `src/components/map/WizardNewsPanel.jsx:116-224`. There the headline prints as `<h4>`, `newsBodyText` prints as the body `<p>` with `title={newsReaderSummary}` (the summary is the TOOLTIP), and up to three `newsReasonPhrases` print as pills.
- **Lit sample:** `SCRATCH/cards.json`, 4,496 cards. **Every body in this run is a lifecycle POOL line.** 0 of 4,496 cards took the governed `familyId → summary` branch (newsBody.js:160). That branch is dark here, so the body never carries a fact: on all 4,496 cards the facts sit only in the hover tooltip. Pool tallies: appliedLight 2,056 · queued 894 · ready 839 · expired 516 · appliedHeavy 172 · default 19 · resolved/ignored 0.
- **Harness caveat (counts are of this world shape):** the sim's regional graph has no named nodes (`h/sim.mjs:181`, `ensureRegionalGraph({ edges, channels })`). So all 2,217 regional-impact cards print the neutral target "A far settlement", and the wave source prints a RAW ID ("come from b", 1,686 tips). The neutral fallback is a deliberate design. The raw id is a real code path, recorded as `defect` at A1·38. The `applied`/`resolved`/`ignored` impact transitions are dark in this run.
- **Design constraints read before proposing:** the wizardNews headline is the METRONOME SUPPRESSION KEY (`wizardNews.js:555-562`) and must stay single-valued per input. heraldDeeds is "ONE AUTHORED FORM PER DEED" by design (`heraldDeeds.js:16-18`). Headline rows therefore carry `needs: vantages (1 of 4)` with the blocker named. Variety would go at render (newsBody / newsVoice), never into the stored headline.
- **Untraced 451 frames:** each family was traced by grepping a fixed fragment. The families written by my files are judged in my rows: wizardNews "A far settlement …" headlines and tips; the newsBody "It bites hard … none can miss it" bodies; deityStanceLane "finds an ear" and "turns on"; heraldDeeds deeds; stressorAftermath "has passed" and "passes into history". The rest are listed by writer under `#### Untraced handed on`.
- **SW-3:** newsBody.js:115 (row 21) and :228 (row 22) are cured on SW-3's lane, not on this base. No newsVoice row is judged here.

#### Counts (A1)
- **Blocks judged: 113.** PASS 39 (of which PASS (label) 11) · REWRITE 70 · `defect` 2 (A1·38, A1·113) · cured on SW-3's lane 2 (A1·9, A1·10).
- **REWRITE by code** (a row can carry several): machinery-leak 24 · no-evidence 24 · mechanism-first 19 · duplicate-surface 18 · mannered 14 · aphorism 4 · retired-word 3 · disconnected 0. Eight no-evidence rows carry "contradicts:" in needs: A1·2, 3, 4, 19, 24, 31, 34, 35. The A1·3/A1·4 contradiction is caused by the `defect` at A1·113.
- **Lit on REWRITE rows:** the sum of the row counts is 16,331. That is an upper bound: the slot rows A1·25, 27 and 28 sit inside the summaries counted at A1·20–22. The body pools alone are all 4,496 cards: every body printed in the run comes from a REWRITE row.
- **Retired word "faction(s)/factional":** 5 tokens in my files' printed-prose literals: heraldDeeds.js:68 ×2, relationshipRulesAdversarial.js:164 ×1, relationshipRulesCore.js:539 ×1 and :820 ×1. Lit in cards.json from my writers: 69 (cold_war_proxy_conflict reason 34 + tooltip 34; npc_defect headline 1). A further 10 lit tokens come from factionCompetition.js (A2). No `${x.faction}` slot in my files.

#### Worst five (A1)
1. **A1·4 / A1·1 (the body pools):** "It is being felt {scope} now." This is the only body any card printed (4,496/4,496). It carries no fact, while the event, the source, the road and the goods hide in a hover tooltip.
2. **A1·40:** "An ally faces a gated obligation to mirror the hostility or cold-war pressure bearing on its partner." It prints 436 times (pill and tooltip), is pure rule documentation, and names nobody.
3. **A1·14:** "${town} feels the trouble spreading from town to town". 1,292 headlines never say what the trouble is, and the pill under each one says "spreading from town to town" again.
4. **A1·42:** "Cold-war third party: ${supportedColdWar.thirdPartyId}." A raw id ("j.") is printed as a pill on 38 cards. It is the purest machinery leak in the partition.
5. **A1·94:** "Weak legitimacy gives cold-war rivals a proxy faction opening." It has engine nouns and the retired word, and it is the same sentence in the pill and the tooltip (68 prints).

- **deityStanceLane.js:** excluded the tuning table, the cooldown keys, the ruleIds and `conversionCause` tokens, and the comments at :1-39.
- **warHomeCosts.js:1-299:** excluded tuning constants and reinforcement/levy maths. There is no prose literal there.
- **mobilizationEffects.js:** excluded `sourceEventType`, the relationship keys and the id templates.
- **stressorAftermath.js:** excluded `HISTORY_EVENT_TYPE` (:1-50; enum values) and `severityWord` (:55-60). The latter prints as the History tab's severity LABEL ("major"), which is PASS (label) by rule and is not a news position.

  - The consumers of the channel `explanation` (religiousContest.js:613, mobilizationEffects.js:209) and of the condition `causes[].reason` strings were not verified. The rows for them (A1·69, A1·92, A1·93, A1·104) are marked dark, with consumers unverified.


### Ledger A2: the other printed world-pulse writers (30 files in part/A2.files, plus the measured proxy line's writer)

- Opened: the law (WIRING-RULINGS §5–§8, STYLE-RULINGS §1–§2 at HEAD), part/A2.printed.json (205 traced frames, 0 untraced), cards.json (4,496 cards) through a small counter (`SCRATCH/cnt.cjs`, regex → field|impactKind counts), lits.json for the 30 files (797 literals), and every printing region of each ruled file read with its slots (the census read: what name/target/label each template holds) — not every helper line of the 22k lines (see Not reached / method note below).
- Positions are judged separately: `headline` (card title, intro), `summary` (tooltip and chronicle body, intro), `reasons[]` (pills, first three print; description position). `proposal.reason` / `condition.causes[].reason` strings are judged only where they print (WorldPulsePanel / HeraldAdjudication show proposal reasons); condition-cause reasons are consumed as state notes and excluded.
- lit = printed in the 2×52-week run with its card count (from cards.json, not the frame file, because the frame file splits names with umlauts into separate frames); dark = not printed here (a lower bound).
- Every headline/summary block is a single template, so every intro row also carries `needs: vantages (1 of 4)` unless noted; I give four-vantage proposals only for the five highest-lit lead pools and a single plain line elsewhere (the lane writes the rest).
- SW-3 (realm-prose) rewrote none of these 30 files (its §4a cites newsVoice, decreeProsePools, traditionProse, causeWalk and others only), so no row here is `cured on SW-3's lane`.
- The measured line "Weak legitimacy gives cold-war rivals a proxy faction opening." (34 cards × reason + tooltip = 68) is written by `src/domain/worldPulse/relationshipRulesAdversarial.js:164` (outside the 30); ruled as row A2·P1 at the end.

#### Counts (A2)
- Blocks judged: **185** (186 rows; A2·42 is a pointer to A2·35, not a separate block). 121 rows lit in the run, 65 dark.
- **PASS 30** (incl. PASS (label): A2·58, 98, 163 part, 171 plain, 84 part, 85 part) · **REWRITE 155** (99 wholly REWRITE; 55 group rows where some variants PASS and named variants REWRITE; A2·22 coded defect + duplicate-surface).
- By code (rows carrying it): mechanism-first 72 · machinery-leak 60 · mannered 29 · no-evidence 17 · duplicate-surface 13 · retired-word 8 · aphorism 7 · disconnected 2 · defect 7 (A2·22 raw target id "committed against f."; A2·34/36/39 sentence opens lower-case on a cause label; A2·104 lower-case tier token; A2·114 "1 occupation weigh"; A2·171 "corrupted corrupted").
- Intro (headline/summary) rows: every one is a single template → `needs: vantages (1 of 4)` throughout; four-vantage proposals written for the top lead pools (A2·2, 27, 44, 52, 56, 95, 107, 125, 146, P1).
- Retired word "faction(s)/factional": **11 prose literals** in these 30 files (stressors.js:89; realmEvents.js:33, 53, 72; factionCompetition.js:811, 812, 848, 985, 1010; stressorDynamics.js:850; stressorGates.js:1019), plus the short label `Faction challenge` (factionCompetition.js:840). Lit in cards.json from these files: **10** (factionCompetition.js:811 ×8, :1010 ×2). The measured proxy line (relationshipRulesAdversarial.js:164, outside the 30) adds **68** lit (34 tooltip + 34 reason). (`FACTION_VERB_PHRASES` keys and `faction_*` kinds are identifiers, not counted.)

#### Worst five (A2)
1. A2·P1 — "Weak legitimacy gives cold-war rivals a proxy faction opening." — 68 printings, retired word plus engine words, both towns unnamed, and the same sentence printed twice on each card.
2. A2·28 — "The compromise crossed covert → revealed; the public arc supersedes the quiet lifecycle." — 139 printings of a state-machine arrow on the most-lit scandal card.
3. A2·169/170 — "civic role favors reform." / "Pressure sat well clear of the gate, and the ambition behind it is hungry." — 206 NPC cards each, archetype tokens and "the gate" in the pills.
4. A2·3 — "{N}'s strategy chooser selected an offensive deployment." — 78 war cards name the algorithm instead of the decision.
5. A2·75 — "Organic settlement drift is conservative: pressure must pass a gate before it can roll." — 52 cards carry a rule comment as a reason; the raw pressure keys beside it (A2·74) already have reader sentences in stressors.js that the pressure cards never use.

#### Excluded (A2)
- `condition.causes[].reason`, `condition.description`/`label` (except A2·166, noted as dossier), graph-channel `explanation` strings (tradeWar.js:584, 606; warDeployment.js:1170–1171), `foodStockpileDeltas`/`populationDeltas` reasons on war outcomes (warDeployment.js:875–885; deploymentReturn.js:421–422), `institutionPatch`/`powerTransfer.cause` keys: consumed as state notes, not printed on the pulse card (I ruled only the ones with a known printing surface: A2·111, 178).
- warDeployment.js:857 `${occupierName} occupation authority` is a power NAME minted into the roster (prints wherever powers print, outside the pulse card) — noted, not coded; it reads as officialese ("Oberthal occupation authority") and the power lanes may want it.
- Enum/word tables used only as slot words and judged inside their host rows (RESISTANCE_WORDS, OCCUPATION_YIELD_WORDS, RETURN_MUSTER/ODDS_WORDS, SIEGE_MATCHUP_WORDS, REINFORCEMENT_*_WORDS, POPULATION_PRESSURE_WORDS, CAUSE_LABEL_OF, REALM_LABELS, stressor labels).
- Dev comments, JSDoc, test-facing ids, `ruleId`/`candidateType`/`conflictTags`, `sourceEventType` tokens, and stressorGates.js:366–388 magic-signal phrases (only slots inside A2·84).
- Frames the seat traced to stressorsCore.js but written in src/domain/worldPulse/stressorAftermath.js (`${label} has passed` :89, `… is over, but it leaves … It will be remembered for a while yet.` :90 — lit 36, `${label} passes into history` :121): not in A2's 30 files; hand to the part that holds stressorAftermath.js. Likewise the war_mobilization, reinforcement_cost and organic-pressure headlines/summaries (heraldDeeds.js / CONDITION_DEEDS) and the NPC deed/aim tables (heraldDeeds.js, eventProse.js).

#### Not reached (A2)
- Every one of the 30 files has its printing positions ruled, but the helper bodies of the largest files were read by region, not whole, within budget: settlementStrategy.js (scoring/belief helpers 200–840), occupation.js (ledger advance 400–1090), warDeployment.js (siege arithmetic 1–650, 890–1150), stressorGates.js (helpers 1–160, 180–400), stressorDynamics.js (counterforce maths 1–370, 520–650), npcAgency.js (1–870, 925–1219), institutionLifecycle.js (1–360, 450–760, 860–1160), factionCompetition.js (1–690). No printing literal from lits.json in those ranges is unruled except the stressorsCore.js:518 default `Resolved by party action` (PASS) and settlementStrategy.js:978–979 condition-cause notes (excluded above).


### Ledger B: the realm's receipt pools and their news pickers

- Opened, on base `2053a809c`: every pool file whole (warReceiptPools, commercial, grammar, sovereignty, information, faith, market and envoyChanceMeeting receipt pools), eventProse.js whole, and the code of every `*News.js` picker and the two reason layers (their docblock comments skimmed, their code and literals read in full), plus `npcAgency.js` (the npc-goal writer), the four consumers that set the stage (`commercialReasons.js`, `treatyLifecycleVoice.js`, `faithNews.js`, `envoyChanceMeetingNews.js`) and `components/map/WarCausalBrief.jsx`. Path shorthand: `wp/` = `src/domain/worldPulse/`. Annex blocks: `WAR`, `LEGACY`, `TRADE`, `GRAMMAR`, `INFORMATION`, `FAITH`, `CHANCE` = `docs/content/RECEIPT_POOLS_<X>.md`, cited by block heading. "code-side" = an eventProse-family pool with no annex block (the walker manifest is `EVENT_PROSE_REGISTRY`).
- LIT vs DARK. I checked every pool against `cards.json` (4,496 cards). The longest fixed fragment of every variant was searched in headline, body, voice, reasons and tip (script: `SCRATCH/b/lit.mjs`, output `SCRATCH/b/lit.txt`). **Only `NPC_GOAL_NEWS` is lit**: 5 cards (4 `npc_goal_culmination`, 1 `npc_goal_rebranch`), giving the 16 frames in `part/B.printed.json`. Every other pool in Part B is DARK in this world, including all 97 war, peace and hegemony pools and all 157 commercial pool paths. The world has wars (165 `war_mobilization` cards), but no WR-2..WR-10 receipt kind, casus receipt, commercial crossing, treaty lifecycle beat, sovereignty sale, envoy errand, chance meeting, faith extinction or wrong-market arrival reached the feed. Dark is a lower bound, measured on one world shape.
- WHERE EACH POOL PRINTS (traced by consumer). WR-2..WR-7 (`dispositionReceipt`, `lineageReceipt`, `warCostReceipt`, `warRulingReceipt`, `warCoalitionReceipt`, `envoyReceipt`) and the sovereignty, commercial-crossing, grammar `treaty_lapsed`/`_default_detected`/`_disclosure_opened` and market pools all fill the card **summary**, so they are LEAD positions under the four-vantage rule. The writer's own `HEADLINE` sets the title and its `REASON` the pill. `faith_last_altar_dark` and `chance_meeting_*` fill the **headline**. The per-type `cc.*` pools ride the crossing card as the second reason pill (`casusReceipt`) and the commercial ledger. The legacy casus pools (`grievance`…`dependency_by_design`, `PEACE_RECEIPTS`, `HEGEMONY_RECEIPTS`, `DECREE_DEFAULT_RECEIPTS`) are persisted on `spatialLedgers.warReasons/peaceReasons` and print as the receipt half of a `WarCausalBrief.jsx :: ReasonRow` ("Type: receipt") beside a treaty or a live siege, not in the news feed. `treaty_age_line`, `treaty_true_state_chip`, `succession_question_open`, `mirror_standing_line` and `commercial_relation_line` are dossier or treaty-panel lines (description positions).
- RUMOUR REACH, corrected: `src/domain/display/rumorPhrasePools.js` (and `rumorFallbackPhrasePoolsEvents.js`) **do not import or consume `warReceiptPools`**. They name it only in a docblock, as the precedent for the data-leaf split (`rumorPhrasePools.js:5`, "the same split warReceiptPools.js makes"), and `rumorPhrasePools.js:38` declares "no imports". So no rumour phrase prints a war receipt line. The only reach beyond the news card is the `WarCausalBrief` reason row above.
- MACHINERY VS IN-WORLD BOOKS. Watching for "the record / the ledger / the book(s)", I separate two uses. A clerk's book a visitor could see is evidence ("The granary book at X names Y in every entry of the lean months", "the factors' book carries X in the column of debts unanswered") and passes. The engine's record narrated as the world's is a leak and is coded `machinery-leak`: "the receipt names…", "the record carries…", "the ledger has scored it", "edge", "minted", "reads {band}", "arithmetic", "optimises".
- Excluded (detail in the last section): thrown errors, veto `detail` codes, ids, tuning-register labels, and the `*_SLOTS` declaration arrays. `ROADS_NEWS` (re-exported by eventProse from `src/data/roadsProse.js`) is not a Part B file and SW-3 touched it. No Part B file is in SW-3's §4a manifest, so no row here is "cured on SW-3".

#### Counts (B)
- Blocks judged: **152** rows (several pools judged together where one verdict covers them, as named in each row). PASS **43** (33 PASS and 10 PASS (label)), REWRITE **108**, defect **1** (B·27).
- REWRITE by code (a row can carry more than one): machinery-leak **87**, mechanism-first **11**, mannered **9** (including the struck tics "arithmetic", "rather than", "breath", "a stranger", "nothing has broken yet"), aphorism **8**, no-evidence **7**, duplicate-surface **2** (B·148, B·152), retired-word **1** (B·70), disconnected **0**.
- LIT: **8 rows** carry all 16 printed frames (B·1–B·4, B·7–B·10, `NPC_GOAL_NEWS`; 5 cards, 4 culmination and 1 rebranch). **6 of the 8 lit rows are REWRITE**, and every one of those six is the same leak: the goal, role, ideal and flaw TOKENS printed in place of words ("Role: civic; goal: win public legitimacy."). The other 144 rows are dark in this world.
- Four-vantage rule (lead pools): about 20 lead pools carry fewer than four vantages, mostly the court/council vantage only (WR-2 disposition, the R3 upswing summaries, calamity, npc_goal). The TR-1 commerce corpus and GR-0 lapse/default pools meet it.
- Retired word "faction(s)/factional" as plain prose in Part B's printed literals: **1** token, `wp/warRulingsNews.js:65` "The installing faction wrote its war demand into the recorded succession." Lit count in cards.json: **0** (none of the 5 lit cards carries it). The `${x.faction}` SLOT, which prints a power's NAME (noted, not coded), appears 12 times: 5 in warReceiptPools (:323, :355, :363, :370, :376), 3 in informationReceiptPools (:68, :70, :122) and 4 in warRulingsNews HEADLINE.

#### Worst five (B)
1. B·65: "${x.npc} optimises a third book: the terms answer ${x.faction}'s needs before ${x.settlement}'s." This pool puts optimiser, ledger and receipt in a ruler's mouth: "optimises", "arithmetic", "the receipt names it", "The web already knows".
2. B·128: "The machinery ran and produced nothing: ${x.counterpart} would not take what ${x.third_party} could bear to give." It names the engine outright, as "the machinery".
3. B·76: "${x.settlement}'s surviving current-episode evidence reads ${x.band}; no lifetime total is invented." This is a design note on bounded evidence printed as the card's lead line.
4. B·79: "One aggregate judgment became separate bilateral shares under the same settlement identifier." "Identifier", "aggregate" and "bilateral" are schema words printed as news.
5. B·4 (LIT): "Role: civic; goal: win public legitimacy." A printed card shows the enum dump as a reason pill, with the de-underscored goal token also quoted in the summary.

#### Excluded (B)
- `wp/envoyChanceMeetingNews.js:132–145`: thrown errors (`chanceMeetingKindRow: … no corpus named …`). `:303–:309`: tuning-register labels ("CANDIDATE, OWNER-UNSIGNED…", "the recorded meeting's Herald severity…"), read by the instrument and never printed.
- `wp/commercialReasonsNews.js` (`…has no annex pool of matching arity`), `wp/marketNews.js:63`, `wp/warCoalitionNews.js:66` ("Coalition refusal prose must cover every closed refusal cause."): thrown errors.
- `wp/warReasons.js:1049` / `wp/peaceReasons.js:727` ("peace-engine gate absent"): veto `detail` strings for code, not the printed VETO_PROSE.
- `*_SLOTS` / `requiredSlots` arrays (commercialReceiptPools.js:268ff, marketReceiptPools MARKET_RECEIPT_SLOTS), the `*_KIND_REGISTRY` rows, the `IMPACT_KIND`/`CROSSING_KIND` maps, ids and seeds: declarations only.
- The band word maps (`BAND_WORD`, `TEMPER_WORD`, `INWARD_WORD`, warCoalitionNews "a real risk / a heavy share…", warCostsNews "by uncertain report"): slot fills, judged inside the lines that print them, plus PASS (label).
- `ROADS_NEWS` (re-exported through `EVENT_PROSE_REGISTRY` from `src/data/roadsProse.js`): not a Part B file, and SW-3's lane rewrote roadsProse rows.
- `wp/proseSelection.js` (named in the brief's area, not in `part/B.files`): the selection kernel, with no literals.

#### Not reached
- None. All 23 files were ruled on or excluded. Method limit: in the 15 `*News.js` / reason-layer / voice files, the long docblock comments were skimmed, while their code and every literal were read. The pool leaves and eventProse.js were read in full.


### Ledger C1: the dark world-pulse remainder, first half (89 files of part/C1.files)

- Triage: every one of the 89 files' literals in SCRATCH/lits.json (1,977 literals) was listed and read (dump at SCRATCH/c1/lits.txt). Files whose literals are design-ledger prose, tuning-register descriptions, throw messages, receipt/kernel log text or stage manifests were excluded with a reason (#### Excluded). None of the 89 files has a printed frame in this run (part/C1 has no printed.json; the seat's trace put no frame here), so EVERY row is `dark` (lit 0); the `needs` cell names the world shape that would light it where the writer's gate says so.
- Consumer trace: for each prose file, its writer was followed to a wizardNews entry (headline = card title, summary = tooltip/chronicle and the card body when `familyId` is set, reasons[] = up to three pills), a proposal (`reasons[]` printed by WorldPulsePanel / HeraldAdjudication), or a non-news surface (TemperamentPicker, SimulationRulesDialog, WatchPanel). A file with no `src/` importer (operationsVoice, envoyTaskCatalog, infiltrationDrift/Depth, routeNetworkCharterEvents via routeNetworkCharter only) is dark by construction and says so.
- Every file ruled on (a PASS or REWRITE row) was read whole once; the files with prose that budget did not reach are listed under `#### Not reached` with their literal counts so a second seat can take them.
- Lead (intro) positions are the headline and the familyId summary of a news card; reasons pills, receipt lines and dialog captions are description positions (single, plain). A lead pool with one fixed frame per event kind gets `needs: vantages (1 of 4)`.
- Proposals read only the writer's own slots (census law); where the writer holds a name it does not print, the proposal uses it and says so in needs.

#### Counts (C1)
- Blocks judged: 106 rows over 18 files read whole (warTerminationCauseTables, treasuryNews, generosityNews, coup, routeNetworkCharterEvents, npcDmVerbRecords, factionCapture, counterIntelSweep, commonsVoiceKernel, seasons, demographicsHerald, espionage/operationsVoice, temperamentPresets, partyImpactKinds, warCoalitionRefusal, peaceTermsDrafting, simulationProfile, navalKernel). Every row is dark (lit 0): no frame from C1's files printed in the 2×52-week run.
- PASS (incl. PASS (label)): 45 · REWRITE: 58 (6 of them split rows, part PASS and part REWRITE) · defect: 3 (C1·28 coup lower-cased name, C1·69 demographic exodus lower-case lead, C1·93 non-aggression receipt missing "for").
- By code (a row may carry two): mannered 21 · machinery-leak 18 · mechanism-first 15 · no-evidence 9 (one with "contradicts:", C1·103) · aphorism 8 · duplicate-surface 7 · retired-word 4 · disconnected 0.
- `needs: vantages (1 of 4)`: every news headline that PASSES is one fixed frame per event kind (30 lead rows); none of C1's lead pools has more than one variant.
- Retired word "faction(s)/factional" in C1's printing prose literals: 10 tokens — commonsVoiceKernel.js:223 (1), partyImpactKinds.js:30–31 (4, two labels and two notes), simulationProfile.js:283 (1), pulseKernel.js:764,772,773 (3, not reached), calamityKernel.js:109 "Multiple criminal factions" (1, an institution catalogue name used as a lookup key, not prose). Lit in cards.json from C1's files: 0 (cards.json holds 79 faction tokens in all, none traced to C1). A further 11 tokens sit in non-printing literals (changeAuthorityPolicy, decisionTier, npcVerdictApply field notes, habitForkRegistry, infiltrationDepth).

#### Worst five (C1)
1. C1·92 — "A victor-aligned seat installed (registration seam): cheap control, brittle control." — an engine seam name printed in a treaty clause, followed by an aphorism.
2. C1·75 — "The records were read before the person was, and the records ended it. Character speaks only for those the paperwork clears." — a struck tic and an aphorism standing where the reason should be.
3. C1·98 — "${humanKey(key)} now runs on its own" — every unphrased rule flag prints its camelCase name ("commons voice now runs on its own").
4. C1·27/28 — "The ${String(incumbentName).toLowerCase()} fell. … and the settlement holds its breath." — a struck tic plus a lower-cased proper name.
5. C1·57 — "The gathering has become a riot-band: an organised crowd, short of rebellion but past all petitioning, …" — the line defines the rung instead of showing the riot.

#### Excluded (C1)
- habitForkRegistry.js (190 literals): the habit-fork registry's `reading` / `close` / disposition rows are design-ledger prose for the chair; no src importer prints them (espionageGauntlet.js names it in a comment only).
- envoyTaskCatalog.js (90): census rationale, owner rows and provenance; the four method-fit lines (:398–423) are design notes citing "the volume"; no src module imports the leaf (its own provenance, :627).
- espionage/infiltrationDrift.js (47), espionage/infiltrationDepth.js (33): charter exits, census rows, provenance and one throw message; no surface.
- operations/operationGrammar.js (40): ledger-ownership citations for each grammar arm; no surface.
- demographicsRates.js (44), faithTuningSurface.js (19), deityFlaws.js (12): tuning-register descriptions and signature provenance read by the tuning walkers (faithDeepening.js reads deityFlaws' FLAW_EFFECTS values, not its notes).
- changeAuthorityPolicy.js (31), decisionTier.js (31): `rationale` strings on policy / drama-class rows, read by tests and certification, never printed.
- pulseStageManifest.js (22), ledgerOwnershipManifest.js (8): stage descriptions and code-citation strings.
- peopleLedger.js (14), bandedStock.js (9): throw messages from conservation and band-ladder validators.
- npcVerdictApply.js :85–111 (the field-note half of its 29): descriptions of which roster fields a verdict clears; its news half (:329–401) is under Not reached.
- treasury.js (10): revenue-line names used as keys to map other files' income labels to receipt kinds.
- calamityKernel.js :104–127 (21 of its 40): institution catalogue names used as lookup keys; its prose half is under Not reached.
- envoyChanceMeeting.js (10): tuning-register row descriptions only.
- simulationProfile.js and the rest of the ruled files were read whole; their non-printing literals (throws, dev comments) are not rows.

### Ledger C1b: the dark world-pulse files C1 triaged but did not reach (28 files of part/C1b.files)

- Triage: every one of the 28 files' literals in SCRATCH/lits.json (774 literals; dump at SCRATCH/c1b/lits.txt) was listed and read; part/C1b has no printed.json and the seat's trace put no frame here, so EVERY row is `dark` (lit 0). The `needs` cell names the world shape that would light a row where the writer's gate says so.
- Reading (a departure from the read-whole doctrine, stated plainly): the 28 files hold 11,753 code lines, more than the budget. realmVerbExecution.js and settlementLifecycleFirstClass.js were read in their code lines (comments skipped, SCRATCH/c1b/rd.sh) through every writer; the other ruled files were read writer by writer, each prose-bearing function whole plus the slot sources it reads. Then the writer of each block was followed to its consumer: a wizardNews entry (headline = card title; summary = tooltip / chronicle, and the card body when `familyId` is set; reasons[] = up to three pills), a proposal (`reasons[]` / summary printed by WorldPulsePanel / HeraldAdjudication), a DM refusal card, or a non-news surface (Daily Life panel, dialog captions).
- C1's conventions kept: lead (intro) positions = the headline and the familyId summary; reason pills, receipt lines, refusal details and dialog captions are description positions (single, plain). A lead pool with one fixed frame per event kind gets `needs: vantages (1 of 4)`.
- Census law: proposals use only the slots the writer already holds (named in the row when the old line did not print them); where a needed fact is not held, `needs` says so instead of inventing it.
- Excluded literals (throws, guard reasons consumed by code, field notes, institution catalogue keys) are listed under `#### Excluded`; files the budget did not reach are under `#### Not reached`.

#### Counts (C1b)
- Blocks judged: 120 (C1b·1–C1b·120), all dark (lit 0); files ruled on: 22 of the 28.
- PASS (incl. PASS (label)): 30 (26 PASS, 4 PASS (label)) · REWRITE: 90 (one split row, C1b·78: label PASS, description REWRITE; several rows rewrite only one arm of a pool and say which).
- By code (a row may carry several): machinery-leak 64 · mannered 24 · aphorism 17 · mechanism-first 14 · duplicate-surface 11 · no-evidence 5 · retired-word 0 · disconnected 0 · defect 6 (C1b·46 and ·48 roamer pills print an origin settlement id; C1b·59 razing verdict pill prints razer/victim ids, razingExecution.js:519; C1b·112 pact-refused turning point prints ids and a trigger key; C1b·113 pact term receipt prints ids; C1b·115 the coalition peace summary reads "leaving the allied fronts standing under ${terms}").
- Struck tics seen: "the record" (C1b·52, ·93, ·94), "a stranger" (C1b·44, fallback name), "… rather than / not …" balances (C1b·14, ·75), "the engine kills no named character, ever" printed as a pill twice (C1b·29, ·50).
- `needs: vantages (1 of 4)`: every lead row that passes (25 headline rows) is one fixed frame per event kind; no lead pool in this part carries a second variant.
- Retired word "faction(s)/factional" in this part's printing prose literals: 0. In non-printing literals: 9 (calamityKernel.js:109 "Multiple criminal factions", an institution catalogue key; npcVerdictApply.js:86–111, 8 roster field notes). Lit in cards.json from these files: 0 (nothing here printed in the run). The `${houseName}` / `${factionName}` slots print a power's name and are not the word.

#### Worst five (C1b)
1. C1b·99 — "A D-4f linked support-goal outcome." — a design-section id printed as the card's only reason.
2. C1b·60 — "the quarrel burns ${heat}: resentment ${round4(resentment)} against a floor of ${baseline}, and a live grievance at ${round4(grievance)}." — a four-decimal number dump on the cruelest card in the feed (a town burned).
3. C1b·25 / C1b·32 — "FORCE_ABANDON: the DM-authority terminal-death verb (dwell-bypassing; resolves through the organic path)." — the verb's enum name and its implementation notes as a reason pill.
4. C1b·29 / C1b·50 — "The last residents disperse with fates UNRESOLVED. The engine kills no named character, ever." — the engine names itself, in capitals, on the death of a town.
5. C1b·59 — "${actorId} took ${victimId} and means to burn it. Only the wicked burn first, and this is a court that will." — prints settlement ids (a defect) and closes on an aphorism.

#### Excluded (C1b)
- relationshipMemory.js (68 of 69): the posture reasons, practical effects, memory summaries and the `emphasis` line feed the AI grounding context (store/aiSlice.js:132 → aiGrounding.js:554), a prompt and not a printed surface; only POSTURE_LABELS print (C1b·40).
- settlementPolitics.js (22): bloc glue `detail`s, tie reasons and bloc receipts go to the pulse receipts and `politicsRead.js` hands the raw glue to the DM projection only; no component renders them (PowerTab.jsx / RealmIntrigue.jsx read no `detail`).
- militaryStrength.js (18): the capacity contributor lines ("Tier provides the structural body of fighters (…% of max tier).") are not rendered: display/armyStrength.js reads only `theoreticalCapacity` into a band phrase. A further consumer (warCapacityReads.js) was not traced; if it prints them, every line is a machinery-leak.
- convergence.js: engagement-move receipts (:665–669) are never read (only `.move` is, :1172); prizeRivalryCasus (:726) has no caller; the "REGISTERED in realmManifest.js" strings (:1372–1398) are registry notes.
- razing.js: the refused-verdict receipts (:475–:521 refusals), the extremity receipt (:375–385), tierFallDescriptor (:658–669), razingSpoils (:828–840) and compareSpoils (:869–872) do not print (razingExecution.js:1098 drops refused verdicts; no consumer reads plan spoils/fall receipts; compareSpoils has no src caller).
- npcVerdictApply.js :85–111 (field notes); calamityKernel.js :104–127 (institution catalogue keys), :249/:251 (throws); settlementLifecycleFirstClass.js :232 aside, its dial label; settlementLifecycleKernel.js :901/:919/:951 (throws), :1246/:1247 dial labels (PASS (label) in kind, not rows); realmVerbExecution.js :169 (unknown-verb guard); infoLure.js :553 (a guard note); supplyWebWarfare.js :188–221 instrument names (used inside C1b·65) and :1030/:1060 gate codes.

#### Not reached
Prose-bearing files left unjudged (literal count in lits.json; a third seat takes these):
pactTriggers (17: belief clauses "This court believes…", "Both courts reckon the web around…"; feeds pact proposal reasons) · sovereigntyIntent (18: sale verdict receipts, "commerce memory reads …", "four-channel outcome memory" — likely machinery-leak if printed) · sovereigntyBundle (15: bundle valuation receipts, "the catalog does not carry") · conquestFeasibility (26: :360–364 is a band dump "(armies … against …, allies …, stores … under … weariness)" and :609/:618 carry "The judgement stands in the record as it was made" — struck tic; slots are `partyId`/`counterpartId`, possibly ids; consumers razingExecution.js:1273 and warTermination.js not traced to a surface) · conquestIntent (15: "has no martial disposition on record", :266 mercy line) · lineageClaim (15: "The family record contradicts a sack, and the claim is stayed." ×5 and "the relief ledger" — machinery words if printed; consumer warReasons.js not traced).
Also not traced to the end: the LIFECYCLE_NEWS headline/summary pools (settlementLifecycleKernel.js imports them), CALAMITY_SUMMARIES / CALAMITY_REASONS, grammarReceipt lines used by realmVerbExecution / pactFormation / infoLure, and sovereigntyTransfer receipts — all live in other parts' files.


### Ledger C1c: the dark world-pulse files C1 triaged as prose-bearing but did not reach (27 files of part/C1c.files)

- Triage: all 290 literals of the 27 files in SCRATCH/lits.json were listed (dump: SCRATCH/c1c/lits.txt) and each was traced to its writer and consumer: a wizardNews entry (headline = card title and lead; summary = tooltip/chronicle; reasons[] = up to three pills), an outcome/proposal reason (WorldPulsePanel), a dossier condition card (activeConditions description / cause), the RealmDashboard sphere line, a treaty term receipt, or a kernel receipts[] that no surface reads (excluded).
- Reading: for each file ruled on, the writer function(s) were read whole with their slot sources (lieClaimClause, tradeArteries, openWindows, ROUTE_GRADES, termLabel, the consumers in occupation.js, brokerageServicesRules.js, RealmDashboard.jsx, realmVerbExecution.js). Budget did not allow a whole-file read of the large kernels (pulseKernel 3,007 lines, informationStatecraft 1,547, npcLadderKernel 1,264, pactRenewal 1,063). For those, every literal region was read in context, but the files were not read end to end. That falls short of the owner's whole-file doctrine and is stated here.
- Lit check: every block's longest fixed fragment was searched in SCRATCH/cards.json (headline, body, voice, reasons, tip). The one lit frame is relationshipRuleHelpers.js:270 "Relations between {N} and {N} …" (383 cards). The seat traced it to part A1 (part/A1.printed.json), so it gets a pointer row (C1c·61) and is not re-judged. Every other row is dark (lit 0). The needs cell names the world shape that would light the row where the writer's gate says so.
- Conventions follow C1. Lead positions are the headline and the familyId summary. Pills, receipt lines, condition descriptions and dashboard lines are description positions. A lead with one fixed frame per event kind gets `needs: vantages (1 of 4)`. Proposals use only the writer's own slots. Where a writer holds only an id, the row says the name must be resolved, and that is a read, not a new fact.
- SW-3 overlap: traditionsKernel's summary and headline phrases are SW-3's traditionProse pools (cured on SW-3's lane, not on base). This ledger judges only the kernel's own reason pills, the ownerBit SW-3 left under What stays, and the tradition_change beat.
- Raw-id defects: eight rows. Four writers would print settlement ids or snake_case tokens to a reader: the upswing bust artery, the converted-alliance term receipt, the council-schism cause, and the shared-enemy pill. The sovereignty receipts print raw ids too, but they ride kernel or stage records. These are coded `defect` alongside the voice verdict.

#### Counts (C1c)
- Blocks: 72 rows. 71 judged, plus 1 pointer row (C1c·61, A1's lit frame, not re-judged).
- PASS (incl. PASS (label)): 18 rows (PASS (label): C1c·27, C1c·44). REWRITE: 49 rows, 12 of them split (part PASS, part REWRITE: C1c·11, 16, 21, 40, 41, 45, 49, 52, 59, 60, 67, 72). Defect-only: 4 rows (C1c·13, 26, 68, 69). Rows carrying a defect: 8 in all (adding C1c·31, 43, 55, 62).
- By code (rows carrying it): machinery-leak 23 · mannered 20 · mechanism-first 14 · aphorism 8 · no-evidence 6 (2 of them contradicts: C1c·55, C1c·71) · duplicate-surface 4 · retired-word 2 · disconnected 0.
- Vantages: 11 lead pools need vantages. Ten are 1 of 4 (C1c·1, 5, 17, 34, 37, 50, 54, 58, 64, 72) and C1c·8 is 2 of 4.
- Retired word "faction(s)/factional": 3 tokens in these files' printed prose literals, all in pulseKernel.js (:764 "a faction reads", :772 and :773 "The ${factionKey} faction reads"). Also two snake_case window ids that print raw through npcLadderKernel.js:1181/1182 ("faction_power_falling", "faction_captured"); these are token leaks, coded under C1c·36. Lit count in cards.json from these files: 0. The 113 "faction" hits in cards.json come from other parts' writers.

#### Worst five (C1c)
1. C1c·57: "The spatial substrate distributed the calamity toll by district adjacency and flammability. WHERE the harm fell, never HOW MUCH (the totals are unchanged); the disaster kind is never asserted (bucket-neutral)." This is a design note printed as a reader's pill.
2. C1c·53: "A catastrophe reset the struck district stocks and stamped a rebirth marker. Buildings resist rapid change EXCEPT catastrophe and rebirth (the fabric law)." The engine explains itself on the card.
3. C1c·36: "A challenge pressed through ${windows} cleared the sustained margin. The ranks swapped. Conservation holds, no title inflation." It prints snake_case window ids and the ladder's invariants.
4. C1c·31: "The ${schism.factionKey} faction reads ${schism.subjectId}'s strength ${schism.bandGap} bands from the seat's; …" The retired word, a raw settlement id and a band count, all in one condition cause.
5. C1c·55: "… the harm running along the crowded, timber-close lots;${sparedClause}" This asserts a fire the writer does not know about, and it ends on a bare semicolon when no district was spared.

#### Excluded (C1c)
- strategicPosture.js (13): POSTURE_TERM_WORDS and the posture/appetite receipts (:122–125, :273–315) compose into pactFormation's answer receipt, which rides advancePeacetimePacts' receipts[]. settlementLifecycleKernel.js:619/664 forwards that as kernel receipts, and no surface prints it. The :161–163 literals are a throw message.
- pactRenewal.js (10): the renewal, renegotiation and lapse receipts (:826–1058) ride the same kernel receipts[], so they do not print. Note that they slot `fromId`/`toId`, which are raw ids, and would be defects if a surface ever showed them. The one printed line, the term receipt at :762, is C1c·26.
- pactProposals.js (openPactProposal receipts :308–345; guardsStated :509): realmVerbExecution.js:459 prints the refusal code through PACT_VETO_PROSE, not this receipt (PACT_VETO_PROSE is C1c·24/25). guardsStated has no reader in components or display.
- demographicsPlans.js (10): the plan close lines (:449–502) ride the plan receipts[], which demographicsKernel.js does not forward (only migrationReceipts). planLine (:642–658, "The work stands at {n} percent") has no importer. Both are dark by construction. If they are ever wired: "percent" and the de-snaked `because` tokens are machinery.
- brokerageServices.js refusal details (:390–448, :597) and brokerageServicesPlant.js refusal details (:425–464): brokerageServicesRules.js:284/343/398 `continue`s past a refused result, so no refusal sentence prints. brokerageServicesPlant.js:80 is a dev comment string.
- institutionStatusModel.js :633–641: orphan-audit reasons, consumed by code only.
- pulseKernel.js :157–161: stage manifest descriptions, a dev register.
- peaceTermsCatalog.js: only the label table is printed (C1c·27). The rest of the file is tuning.


### Ledger C2: the dark world-pulse remainder, second half (89 files of part/C2.files)

- Triage: every one of the 89 files' literals was pulled from SCRATCH/lits.json (406 lines). Each prose literal was then traced by grep from its writer to a surface: the news card (WizardNewsPanel through newsBody.js: headline = title, summary = tooltip or body when the entry carries `familyId`, reasons = pills), the proposal card (WorldPulsePanel OutcomeCard: headline, summary, reasons), WarCausalBrief (war and peace reason receipts), stressor detail (WorldPulseData `originContext.reason`), pressure reasons (pressureModel, which feeds candidate reasons and then card pills), and dossier records (institution `description`, history `description`, roster `desc`). A literal whose field has no reader on base (grep over components/, domain/display/ and the news builders) is excluded below, with the field named.
- Read: I read every writer function this ledger rules on whole, with its file header and the helper it calls. For the large files (momentum, beliefMap, applyWorldPulse, armyTransitKernel, roadsKernel, npcGrowthKernel, envoyChanceMeetingStage, npcDmVerbs, supplyKernel) I read the writer region plus its consumer path, not the whole file. That falls short of the owner's whole-file doctrine for those nine files, and I say so here rather than claim more.
- Lit: 0 for every row but C2·42. cards.json was grepped for each frame's distinctive words ("climbs down", "misjudgment", "Plague reaches", "bought story", "half-blind", "winds down", "Pressure builds", "oath sworn", …), and every hit was a coincidence from another writer. One exception: Two exceptions: C2·13's slot word list prints 3 times inside warDeployment's lit line, a frame that belongs to another part; and C2·42's hierarchy_cascade writer printed 1 card, which the per-impactKind check found after my word grep had missed it.
- Dark shapes, where they can be told: demographics on (C2·6–7), war with fog or beliefs on (C2·3–5, 33–35), information-statecraft brokerage (C2·22–25), reputation race (C2·20–21), succession of a treaty holder (C2·27–30), the tempo governor active (C2·37), the roads mover with hostages (C2·38), NPC growth (C2·39–41), a vassal cascade (C2·42), moral-institution founding (C2·44). C2·52 has no caller on base.
- Not re-audited: newsVoice.js and the SW-3 rows. None of my files appear in SW-3's record (I grepped every basename against its §4a; the only hit was the generic word "chronicle").
- Proposals change no slot. Wherever a proposal would need a fact the writer does not read, the `needs` cell names that read.

#### Counts (C2)
- Blocks judged: 56 (C2·1–56).
- PASS (including PASS (label)): 28 (PASS 22, PASS (label) 6).
- REWRITE: 27. Of those, 3 also carry a defect (C2·25, C2·26, C2·52). C2·53 is a defect only.
- By code (a row can carry several): machinery-leak 15 · mannered 6 · aphorism 4 · no-evidence 3 (C2·8 and C2·15 are contradictions) · mechanism-first 2 · duplicate-surface 2 · retired-word 1 · disconnected 0 · defect 4.
- Lead pools under four vantages (needs): 12 headline positions, all at 1 of 4. Ten are flagged in their own rows (C2·1, 3, 16, 22, 28, 29, 30, 33, 36, 39). Two are headlines carried as notes inside a summary row: the wind-down headline (C2·31) and the cascade headline (C2·42).
- Retired word "faction(s)/factional": 1 token in my files' prose literals ("A rival faction", applyWorldPulseFactionRoster.js:144), on a field with no reader. Lit count in cards.json: 0.
- Lit: 1 block (C2·42, the hierarchy_cascade card, 1 card in cards.json: its headline frame and its duplicated summary and pill). C2·13's slot words also print 3 times inside another part's lit frame. Every other row is dark.

#### Worst five (C2)
1. C2·26: "Succession oath decision for {settlementId}". A raw settlement id would print as the title of both a DM proposal and a news card.
2. C2·40: "The years have left their mark on {npc} of {town}: after siege_survived, betrayal, they have grown cautious." Snake_case engine signals land inside the one sentence about a named person.
3. C2·37: "The tempo governor is pacing their arrival." The engine names its own governor, under an aphorism headline ("Pressure builds beneath the calm").
4. C2·52: "Deliberation verdict dispatch_and_wait for demand {demandId}." A pure engine dump as a proposal reason, with raw ids in the headline (dark, not wired).
5. C2·15: "Starving, discredited, and out of hope, the defenders capitulated rather than be stormed." It states hunger and disgrace the writer never read, and it carries the struck "rather than".

#### Excluded (C2)
- No reader of the field on base. Each is a receipt or record field that no component, display module or news builder prints (grep):
  - Demographics step receipts: `line`, `land` and `reason` in demographicsKernel.js, demographicsMigration.js, demographicsPushPull.js (driver `guard` words), demographicsLadder.js (grade `reason`, consumers read `.grade` only) and demographicsLand.js (landLine → demographic_plan_opened receipt). demographicsHerald writes its own prose from the receipt fields.
  - populationHistory `reason` in migrationKernel.js:357, tierOutcomeApply.js:368 and lineageMemberBirth.js:245. Its consumers (trendLens, WhatChangedPanel, OverviewTab) read numbers.
  - tierOutcomeApply.js `removedReason`, `remnantReason`, `institutionHistory.reason` and entrepotKernel.js `builtReason` and history reason. The entrepôt `foundings` receipts are returned and never read.
  - Impairment `description`: corruptionImpair.js, blockadeTransport.js, magicSubstitutionReagents.js:373 and the supplyKernel.js fallback "Supply-starved…". The status.js docstring promises UI/PDF; I found no reader.
  - Attrition `reasons` (attrition.js:257): warDeployment.js:577 and :595 keep only `record`.
  - warPeaceDecision.js `reason`: warRulingsEvidence does not carry it.
  - conquestFeeds.js `causes[].reason`: worldState.conquestFeeds is read for numbers (institutionLifecycle.js:683). These reasons would print raw ids (c.takenId, c.victorId) if ever surfaced: a latent defect.
  - canonRelationshipImpact.js edge-evidence and channel-bundle reasons, relationshipEdgeSeed.js:103 edge reason, applyWorldPulseRelationshipGraph.js:90 (coalition payload reason "bilateral war edge", channel bundle only).
  - pactAmendment.js receipts (raw aId/bId; pact_broken_by_war receipts unread), peaceTermsSale.js term and treaty `receipts` (raw ids), peaceTermsDocument.js fraying `line` (treatyLine is not read by any component), treatyBreach.js ending receipts.
  - sovereigntyReach.js and sovereigntyMarketStage.js `receipts` (raw ids plus "ticks"; returned on the receipts array only).
- Covert-errand receipts with no reader: espionageDoctrine.js, espionageDoctrineStage.js (dispatchCadenceFor has no caller), espionageGauntlet.js (captorLeniencyRead has no caller) and espionageTap.js (standoff and tap receipts ride the errand record). envoyPulse and glossary do not print them.
- No caller on base: convergenceReactive.js (reactiveResponse), routeNetworkDecayLifecycle.js (reviveHiddenCorridors; its route-revival headline and summary are dark-dead), routeNetworkConsumersInterdiction.js (severanceReasons), magicSubstitution.js (magicDependencySentence; its catalog name at :176 is an id key), magicFormsPractitioner.js (ensureTiedPractitioner and mintTiedPractitioner). The last would mint an NPC named "The Hedge wizard of {cid-slug}": a latent defect. Also steadingTopography.js (LANDFORM_PLACE_NAME unused).
- Catalog keys, not prose: magicForms.js (MAGIC_FORM_CATALOG institution names used for matching), secondOrderBelief.js (banned-word list), npcDmVerbs.js:334 ("{kind} at {settlementId}" override token on a DM receipt).
- Errors, guards, registries and dev strings: residueStripGuard.js, bandFamilies.js, lawBandModulation.js, habit/habitCurve.js, habit/habitVocabulary.js, warStance.js, contributionLedger.js, worldPulseFates.js, relationshipCompatibility.js (validateRelationship messages; no UI consumer), faithField.js, envoyChanceMeetingStage.js and npcGoalBranches.js (tuning-registry notes), envoyInbound.js:238, houseActs.js:189 and mediationPressure.js:310 (guard notes), momentum.js:1081 (registry note).
- chronicle.js:138 is an instruction to the AI chronicle edge function, not printed to a person.
- No overlap with SW-3: none of these files appear in its record.

#### Not reached
- I did not read these large files whole, only their writer regions and consumer paths: momentum.js, beliefMap.js, applyWorldPulse.js, armyTransitKernel.js, roadsKernel.js, npcGrowthKernel.js, envoyChanceMeetingStage.js, npcDmVerbs.js, supplyKernel.js. A whole read could turn up prose built from template pieces that lits.json's ≥3-word filter missed.


### Ledger D: the map's words (src/components/map, 86 files)

- **Opened (read to the render, through a filtered view that drops pure style-prop lines):** the brief's named files: LiveWarStatus, ChroniclersLetterPanel, heraldFeed.js, WorldPulsePanel, WorldPulseData.js, WorldPulsePrimitives, HeraldAdjudication, HeraldCommandBody, AdvanceReport, ChronicleScrollback, heraldWanderers.js, HeraldWanderers, WandererVerbControls, RealmIntrigue, RelationshipChronicleSection, WorldMapToolbar (help/undo copy), RealmVerbComposer, WizardNewsPanel (chrome), SimulationRulesAxes (all copy tables), SimulationRulesDialog (toggles, presets, notes). Every other file was ruled from `lits.json` (1,154 prose-shaped literals across the 86) plus a line-context read of each prose-shaped literal; files in the D·106 group were NOT read whole (an honest gap against the read-whole doctrine).
- **Lit sample:** the seat's trace gave D only two printed frames (both the war_drain reason, 22 lines), and that writer is `src/domain/worldPulse/warHomeCosts.js`, an A1 file (D·1 is judged but owned by A1). The one D-owned block that prints on every news card is the lifecycle pill in WizardNewsPanel (D·79, 4,496 cards, counted from cards.json `kind`). Everything else in D is `dark` here: the soak renders only NewsEntry, not the World Pulse / Herald / dashboard surfaces.
- **Measured, not assumed:** `newsReasonPhrases({reasons:['time bounded stressor']})` returns the string unchanged (D·24); `humanizeToken('applied')` → "applied" (D·79); the lifecycle words' meanings read from newsBody.js BODY_POOLS and worldPulseFeedCuration.js:94/221.
- **SW-3:** the one SW-3 touch in components/map (ChroniclersLetterPanel.jsx:118) still prints the old line at this tip; one row (D·9) says so, no proposal. REALM_CONTEST_RECORD_* (domain/display/warStatus.js) is SW-3's and not judged.
- **DM-tool tier:** the §69.3 in-file allowances (WorldPulsePanel "tick 2", LiveWarStatus "tick 1", ChroniclersLetterPanel "tick 1") were honoured for instrument labels (D·17, D·21) but not where the calendar humanizer is already imported and a plain word does the job (D·5, D·8); those proposals re-record the allowance count.
- **Not a vantage pool:** no D block leads a news card or chronicle entry (the leads are domain-written), so no `needs: vantages` is raised; the Herald fallback headlines are section labels the writer holds nothing else for.

#### Counts (D)
- Blocks judged: **106** rows (incl. 1 SW-3 pointer and 1 A1-owned trace row). PASS **36** (18 PASS, 18 PASS (label)); REWRITE **69**; cured on SW-3's lane **1**.
- By code (a row may carry two): machinery-leak **58** · mannered **14** · aphorism **3** · duplicate-surface **3** · retired-word **3** · no-evidence **2** (one is a contradiction: D·75) · mechanism-first 0 · disconnected 0 · defect 0.
- Lit vs dark: lit **2 rows** (D·1: 22 lines, A1's writer; D·79: 4,496 cards) plus D·80 PASS (label) lit on 4,496; every other row dark in this run.
- **Retired word "faction(s)/factional":** in labels/headings **3** tokens (SimulationRulesDialog.jsx:63 "Factions"; WorldPulseData.js:86 "Faction"; RealmIntrigue.jsx:67 "Factions & conspiracies"); in prose **0**. None of the three prints in cards.json (the 56 cards there that carry the word come from domain writers outside D). Identifiers (factionId, factionName, npcLadder.factions) and comments are not counted.

#### Worst five (D)
1. D·79 — MetaPill "applied" / "queued" / "ready" / "expired": the pipeline's state word, on every one of 4,496 printed news cards.
2. D·47 — "Causal links are recorded from the engine’s provenance ledger where marked, inferred from shared entities otherwise; click any receipt to find it on the map.": four engine words in one help line.
3. D·33 — "The authoritative proposal is recorded as dismissed.": the DM's confirmation speaks the read model, not the realm.
4. D·75 — "Each advance runs a pulse whose changes wait for your review.": wrong under Routine or Full autonomy (contradicts SimulationRulesAxes.jsx:43).
5. D·39 — "No recorded condition has been promoted above the routine record.": "the record" twice and "promoted" on the morning brief's empty state.

#### Excluded (D)
- RealmItemShadowDiagnostics.jsx: a default-off internal diagnostic ("Internal, read-only accounting… this shadow model performs no writes"); not a reader surface.
- heraldCommandSourceParity.js: `owner:` strings are component names in a routing table, never rendered.
- heraldCommandSession.js (:142 "the previous Herald view"), HeraldMobileCompanion.jsx, WarCausalBrief.jsx, LabelsLayer.jsx, QuickInspector.jsx, WorldMapTour.jsx, AnnotateToolbar.jsx: their literals are CSS values, fonts, or one short label already covered by a sibling row (WarCausalBrief prints domain prose only).
- RoadsLayer.jsx :118–127 and TerrainToolbar.jsx :45/67: console/log lines.
- aria-label / sr-only text: PerspectiveStandings TERM_HELP, RelationshipChronicleSection TERM_HELP, NpcTrailSection :132, WorldMapToolbar :703, WorldMapStage :247–248 (sr-only), TimelapsePanel :167, RoadScenePanel :165, ChroniclersLetterPanel :140/146, PantheonPanel :234.
- CSS-shaped literals ("1px solid {…}", "repeat(auto-fit, …)", transitions, keyframes) in every file.

#### Not reached
- No file is left unjudged. But 56 files were ruled from the literal inventory and line context, not read whole: the D·106 group (35 files), plus PerspectiveStandings, PantheonPanel, WarResolveSection, RealmDashboard, RealmDocket, RealmForecast, RealmComparisons, NpcTrailSection, HeraldSceneContext, HeraldRemembrance, heraldRegister.js, AutoplacementConsent, RoadScenePanel, WorldMapStage, WorldMapTourSteps.js, TimelapsePanel, SettlementPalette, LayersPanel, WhileYouWereAway, CausalityPopup, HeraldBody. A second seat that wants the doctrine's whole-file read should start with HeraldBody, RealmDashboard and AutoplacementConsent, which carry the most prose.


### Ledger D2: the map's words, the whole-file confirmations

- **What was read whole:** the 56 files ledger-D's line 141 names (the D·106 group's 35 files + the 21 named files), every line, in a view that only strips indentation and drops lines carrying no words (bare brackets, closing tags, and, in the last nine files, style-prop-only lines with no quoted phrase); every comment, import, literal and template line was read. Base tip 2053a809c; no repo file changed.
- **Unit and scope:** for every ledger-D row that cites one of the 56 files (D·83–D·106, plus the two rows whose `needs` cite a twin inside them: D·36 → HeraldJudgmentPointer.jsx:40, D·71 → NpcTrailSection.jsx:76) a CONFIRMED / REVERSED line; then every person-visible block ledger-D missed as a new row D2·n.
- **Lit sample:** none of the 56 files is rendered by the soak (it renders WizardNewsPanel :: NewsEntry only), so every row here is `dark`; cards.json carries no D2 block.
- **Excluded while reading (non-printing or already excluded by D):** aria-label / sr-only / aria-live announcer text (KeyboardPlacementControl :71/:150/:180, WorldMapStage :247–248, PerspectiveStandings + RelationshipChronicle TERM_HELP, NpcTrailSection :132, RealmLockedGate :128, TimelapsePanel :117/:124/:167, HeraldStrip/HeraldGazetteer/RealmInspector aria-labels); raw error strings passed through (`err.message` in KeyboardPlacementControl :131, AutoplacementConsent :145, WhileYouWereAway :123, WorldMapStage :347); dev comments; enum keys; the `Unknown section.` dev fallback (HeraldBody :379).
- **Census reads made for proposals:** envoy purpose kinds read from src/domain/worldPulse/roadsKernel.js:820–993 (embassy, observance, trade, diplomacy, dominion, verification); regional channel types from src/domain/region/graph.js:47–59.

#### Counts (D2)
- **Ledger-D rows checked: 26** (D·36, D·71, D·83–D·106). **CONFIRMED 25** (of them D·89 with its :33 note reversed, D·93 and D·103 with corrected proposals; verdicts and codes unchanged). **REVERSED 1:** D·106's group PASS, reversed for 10 of its 35 files (25 hold).
- **New rows: 20**, all REWRITE, all dark. By code (a row may carry two): machinery-leak **15** · no-evidence **3** (all contradictions: D2·8, D2·10, D2·16) · duplicate-surface **2** · mannered **2** · aphorism 0 · mechanism-first 0 · retired-word 0 · disconnected 0 · defect 0.
- **Retired word "faction(s)/factional" in the 56 files:** prose **0** · labels **0** · comments 1 (HeraldHeadline.jsx:85) · identifiers 5 (NpcTrailSection.jsx:18/:44–47 `npcLadder.factions`, WarResolveSection.jsx:119/:121 `governingFaction`) · slot 1 (WarResolveSection.jsx:121 prints `governingFaction.name`, a power's NAME: noted, not coded). None prints in cards.json. Related: D2·16, where the cure's word "power" already labels a deity tier.

#### Worst five (D2)
1. D2·19 — "queued" / "applied" / "resolved" / "ignored" / "expired": D·79's pipeline words, again, as filter chips beside "Regional impacts".
2. D2·12 — "Recorded reference: {reference}": a raw id printed as a cause, with three more id rows beneath it.
3. D2·8 — "When a war ends by a negotiated peace, its terms are recorded here": the same panel files pacts made in peace and sales.
4. D2·14 — "Ruled by the realm: {outcomeId}": an engine key as the answer to how a town fell.
5. D2·2 — "The realm will drift and may surface proposals to review. This undo is available only for the current session.": two engine verbs and an undo nobody mentioned, in the dialog before every advance.

#### Excluded (D2)
- aria-label / sr-only / aria-live announcer strings in the 56 files (listed in the method bullets); raw `err.message` / mapError pass-throughs; dev comments; enum keys; HeraldBody.jsx:379 "Unknown section." (an unreachable dev fallback).


## What stays

- Every PROPOSED line above is the owner's to veto and the chair's to rule; this seat applied none.
- The remainder in §4 (C1b's six files; the region-read rows) goes to a second seat by the intake rule.
- The defects in §3 are code, not style: each wants its own red-first fix lane.
- The receipt-pool REWRITEs land through their annexes (`docs/content/RECEIPT_POOLS_*.md`), which the walkers re-derive; a hand edit of the `.js` pool reds.
- SW-3 REALM-PROSE's cures (the crier quote, newsBody's two rows, ChroniclersLetterPanel.jsx:118) are not in this base; they print here as the old text until that lane merges.
